<h2 class="sow-headline">Trainee Teachers</h2></div></div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_text" data-index="11" id="panel-35751-2-2-1"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-2-2-1"> <div class="textwidget">With the right tools and support, current <b>Trainee Teachers</b> will become awesome teachers. Through video based self-reflection and collaboration with their mentor, teachers get the objective, actionable feedback they need. Find out more about how you can harness the power of lesson capture to empower all of your trainees.</div>

<h2 class="sow-headline">Senior Leaders</h2></div></div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_text" data-index="14" id="panel-35751-3-0-1"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-3-0-1"> <div class="textwidget">Swivl Teams was designed specifically to help Senior Leaders transform and retain their teachers by creating a culture of feedback and support in their organisation. With the new <b>Swivl Teams</b>, video based collaboration is easier, faster, and more secure than ever before.

<h2 class="sow-headline">What is Swivl?</h2><p class="sow-sub-headline"> <a href="https://www.swivl.com/how-to-use/">
					Learn more about how Swivl works</a></p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="panel-grid panel-has-style" id="pg-35751-7"><div class="panel-row-style panel-row-style-for-35751-7" style="padding: 0px 0; "><div class="panel-grid-cell" id="pgc-35751-7-0"><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-image panel-first-child" data-index="22" id="panel-35751-7-0-0"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-7-0-0"><div class="so-widget-sow-image so-widget-sow-image-default-7877d6771435">
<div class="sow-image-container">
<img alt="" class="so-widget-image" height="557" sizes="(max-width: 1020px) 100vw, 1020px" src="https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/alpha.png" srcset="https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/alpha.png 1020w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/alpha-300x164.png 300w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/alpha-768x419.png 768w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/alpha-480x262.png 480w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/alpha-830x453.png 830w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/alpha-230x126.png 230w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/alpha-350x191.png 350w" width="1020"/>
</div>
</div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-headline panel-last-child" data-index="23" id="panel-35751-7-0-1"><div class="so-widget-sow-headline so-widget-sow-headline-default-8b1378c28296"><div class="sow-headline-container">
<h5 class="sow-headline">Record and Share</h5><p class="sow-sub-headline">The Swivl robot rotates to follow the teacher automatically. It connects to multiple Markers for recording audio throughout the room and uses the tethered iPad (or mobile device) for recording video. It’s easy for teachers to setup, start/stop recording, and upload to their Swivl account.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="panel-grid-cell panel-grid-cell-empty" id="pgc-35751-7-1"></div><div class="panel-grid-cell" id="pgc-35751-7-2"><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-image panel-first-child" data-index="24" id="panel-35751-7-2-0"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-7-2-0"><div class="so-widget-sow-image so-widget-sow-image-default-7877d6771435">
<div class="sow-image-container">
<img alt="" class="so-widget-image" height="591" sizes="(max-width: 984px) 100vw, 984px" src="https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/2-pic@3x.png" srcset="https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/2-pic@3x.png 984w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/2-pic@3x-300x180.png 300w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/2-pic@3x-768x461.png 768w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/2-pic@3x-480x288.png 480w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/2-pic@3x-830x499.png 830w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/2-pic@3x-230x138.png 230w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/2-pic@3x-350x210.png 350w" width="984"/>
</div>
</div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-headline panel-last-child" data-index="25" id="panel-35751-7-2-1"><div class="so-widget-sow-headline so-widget-sow-headline-default-8b1378c28296"><div class="sow-headline-container">
<h5 class="sow-headline">View and Collaborate</h5><p class="sow-sub-headline">From the teacher’s Swivl account, you can securely view the video for self-reflection, share with a colleague for peer feedback using time stamped comments, or share with a coach for rubric evaluation. It’s easy to use and completely secure inside the Swivl platform.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="panel-grid panel-has-style" id="pg-35751-8"><div class="siteorigin-panels-stretch sydney-stretch panel-row-style panel-row-style-for-35751-8" data-stretch-type="full" style="padding: 0px 25px; margin-left: -25px; margin-right: -25px; border-left: 0px; border-right: 0px;"><div class="panel-grid-cell" id="pgc-35751-8-0"><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-image panel-first-child panel-last-child" data-index="26" id="panel-35751-8-0-0"><div class="so-widget-sow-image so-widget-sow-image-default-d6014b76747a">

<h2 class="sow-headline">Team based video collaboration</h2></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="panel-grid panel-has-style" id="pg-35751-10"><div class="panel-row-style panel-row-style-for-35751-10" style="padding: 0px 0; "><div class="panel-grid-cell" id="pgc-35751-10-0"><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-image panel-first-child" data-index="28" id="panel-35751-10-0-0"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-10-0-0"><div class="so-widget-sow-image so-widget-sow-image-default-4b231843ec72">
<div class="sow-image-container">
<img alt="" class="so-widget-image" height="265" sizes="(max-width: 360px) 100vw, 360px" src="https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/1pic-2.png" srcset="https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/1pic-2.png 360w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/1pic-2-300x221.png 300w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/1pic-2-230x169.png 230w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/1pic-2-350x258.png 350w" title="1pic (2)" width="360"/>
</div>
</div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-headline" data-index="29" id="panel-35751-10-0-1"><div class="so-widget-sow-headline so-widget-sow-headline-default-f7479ef4b0a1"><div class="sow-headline-container">
<h5 class="sow-headline">1. Record and Share</h5><p class="sow-sub-headline">The teacher controls when recording starts and ends. The finished recording is quickly uploaded to Swivl web where the teacher can securely view and share it with a coach, mentor, or supervisor.</p></div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-button panel-last-child" data-index="30" id="panel-35751-10-0-2"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-10-0-2"><div class="so-widget-sow-button so-widget-sow-button-wire-1e9a74444690"><div class="ow-button-base ow-button-align-left">
<a class="ow-icon-placement-left ow-button-hover" href="https://apps.apple.com/us/app/swivl/id805185573" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">
<span>
			
			Mobile app		</span>
</a>
</div>
</div></div></div></div><div class="panel-grid-cell" id="pgc-35751-10-1"><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-image panel-first-child" data-index="31" id="panel-35751-10-1-0"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-10-1-0"><div class="so-widget-sow-image so-widget-sow-image-default-4b231843ec72">
<div class="sow-image-container">

</div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-headline" data-index="32" id="panel-35751-10-1-1"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-10-1-1"><div class="so-widget-sow-headline so-widget-sow-headline-default-f7479ef4b0a1"><div class="sow-headline-container">
<h5 class="sow-headline">2. View and Collaborate</h5><p class="sow-sub-headline">Watch video of yourself that you recorded for self-reflection. Then share with a trusted colleague or coach to get objective feedback with context of specific moments in the video.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-button panel-last-child" data-index="33" id="panel-35751-10-1-2"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-10-1-2"><div class="so-widget-sow-button so-widget-sow-button-wire-1e9a74444690"><div class="ow-button-base ow-button-align-left">

			Swivl web sign up		</span>
</a>
</div>

<h5 class="sow-headline">3. Better Performance</h5><p class="sow-sub-headline">When teachers are part of a Team, they get help and feedback that are essential for building a culture of feedback. Teams also give senior leaders the data and insights they need to build a successful programme.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-button panel-last-child" data-index="36" id="panel-35751-10-2-2"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-10-2-2"><div class="so-widget-sow-button so-widget-sow-button-wire-1e9a74444690"><div class="ow-button-base ow-button-align-left">

<h2 class="sow-headline">Team Improvement</h2><p class="sow-sub-headline">The unique combination of hardware and software in the classroom has proven essential for effective and sustainable video observation programs. <h5 class="sow-headline">Higher Ed Lectures</h5><p class="sow-sub-headline">Usually when trying to record a professor and the presentation at the same you can only see one of those clearly. With synchronised Screencasting, professors can clearly record themselves and the presentation at the same time</p></div></div></div></div><div class="panel-grid-cell" id="pgc-35751-15-1"><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-image panel-first-child" data-index="45" id="panel-35751-15-1-0"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-15-1-0"><div class="so-widget-sow-image so-widget-sow-image-default-4b231843ec72">

<h5 class="sow-headline">Research</h5><p class="sow-sub-headline">Researchers require high quality video and audio data with an advanced level of storage security. With multi-audio and multi-camera recording, Swivl helps researchers capture the data crucial to their work.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="panel-grid-cell" id="pgc-35751-15-2"><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-image panel-first-child" data-index="47" id="panel-35751-15-2-0"><div class="panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-35751-15-2-0"><div class="so-widget-sow-image so-widget-sow-image-default-4b231843ec72">
<div class="sow-image-container">
<img alt="" class="so-widget-image" height="464" sizes="(max-width: 708px) 100vw, 708px" src="https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/enterprise@2x.png" srcset="https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/enterprise@2x.png 708w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/enterprise@2x-300x197.png 300w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/enterprise@2x-480x315.png 480w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/enterprise@2x-230x151.png 230w, https://wp-cdn.swivl.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/enterprise@2x-350x229.png 350w" width="708"/>

			<input type="button" class="nf-next" value="{{{ data.nextLabel }}}" />
		</li>
		<# } #>
	</ul>
</script>
<script id="tmpl-nf-mp-breadcrumbs" type="text/template">
	<ul class="nf-breadcrumbs">
		<# _.each( data.parts, function( part, index ) { #>
		<li class="{{{ ( data.currentIndex == index ) ? 'active' : '' }}} {{{ ( part.errors ) ? 'errors' : '' }}}">
			<a href="#" class="nf-breadcrumb" data-index="{{{ index }}}">{{{ ( part.errors ) ? '' : '' }}} {{{ part.title }}}</a>
		</li>
		<# } ); #>
	</ul>
</script>
<script id="tmpl-nf-mp-progress-bar" type="text/template">
    <div class="nf-progress-container">
        <div class="nf-progress" style="width: {{{ data.percent }}}%;"></div>
    </div>
</script> <script id="nf-tmpl-cell" type="text/template">
            <nf-fields></nf-fields>
        </script>
<script id="nf-tmpl-row" type="text/template">
            <nf-cells></nf-cells>
        </script>

<script>
    var toggleItem = (function () {
        var toggleButton = document.querySelectorAll('.js-toggle-features-content');
        var toggleClassName = 'b-plan__toggle-content--icon-down';

        for (var i = 0; i < toggleButton.length; i++) {
            toggleButton[i].addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
                this.classList.toggle(toggleClassName);
            }, false);
        }
    })();
